         <a href=\"/en/dept/local-services\">Local Services</a>\r\n\r\n                    </li>\r\n                    <li class=\"breadcrumb-item\">\r\n                                <span>Fire Safety Burn Bans</span>\r\n\r\n                    </li>\r\n        </ol>\r\n    </nav>\r\n</div>\r\n    <main id=\"maincontent\" class=\"add-scroll-top-margin\">\r\n\r\n        <div class=\"\">\r\n            \r\n            \r\n\r\n\r\n<div class=\"  pb-medium border-bottom-overcast \">\r\n    <div class=\"two-col-container container\">\r\n        <div class=\"row\">\r\n            <div class=\"col-md-12 col-lg-8\">\r\n                \r\n<header class=\"container\">\r\n    <div class=\"page-heading\">\r\n<h1>Fire Safety Burn Bans</h1><div class=\"summary-text\"><p>The King County Fire Marshal issues <strong>Fire Safety Burn Bans</strong> for unincorporated King County in response to hot and/or dry conditions, in coordination with King County cities and other county and state agencies.</p></div>    </div>\r\n</header><div id=\"toc-links\"></div>\r\n            </div>\r\n            <aside class=\"col-md-12 col-lg-4 right-rail\">\r\n                \r\n<div class=\"box bg-overcast\">\r\n\r\n    \r\n    <!-- STAGE 1 BURN BAN -->\n<!-- updated 4/22/2024 by anna.clemenger@kingcounty.gov -->\n<div style=\"text-align: center;\">\n<p>Current status:<br />\n<span class=\"h3\"><a href=\"#toc-Stage-2-burn-ban\" style=\"text-decoration: none; color: #ce4a2e;\"><strong>Stage 2 Fire Safety Burn Ban</strong></a></span></p>\n<p><a href=\"#toc-Stage-2-burn-ban\">Restrictions apply</a></p>\n</div>\r\n</div>\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\n<div class=\"rich-text\"><p><strong>Related documents:</strong></p>\n<p><a href=\"https://cdn.kingcounty.gov/-/media/king-county/depts/local-services/permits/building-land-use-permits/f/fire-hazards-vegetation-or-exterior-property-areas.pdf?rev=193c1949f94c4ef7b17128572d610ff2&amp;hash=D058A0CC245A47FC69B3723D061DFED3\" class=\"link-emphasis link-icon-picture_as_pdf\">Fire hazards &ndash; vegetation or exterior property areas</a></p></div>\r\n            </aside>\r\n        </div>\r\n    </div>\r\n</div>\r\n        </div>\r\n        <div class=\"\">\r\n            \r\n\r\n<aside class=\"highlight-banner highlight-banner-primary\" aria-labelledby=\"The-sale-and-use-of-consumer-fireworks-are-prohibited-in-unincorporated-King-County-title\">\r\n    <div class=\"container\">\r\n        <div class=\"row align-items-center\">\r\n\r\n                        <h2 class=\"col-sm-4 highlight-banner-title h3\" id=\"The-sale-and-use-of-consumer-fireworks-are-prohibited-in-unincorporated-King-County-title\">\r\n                            The sale and use of consumer fireworks are prohibited in unincorporated King County\r\n                        </h2>\r\n\r\n            <div class=\"col highlight-banner-text\">\r\n                <div class=\"rich-text\"><strong><a href=\"https://dls-permit.dynamics365portals.us/submit-case/\">Report fireworks or burning violations online</a>*</strong> or call:\n<ul>\n<li><strong>206-848-0800</strong> to report fireworks violations</li>\n<li><strong>206-296-6675</strong> to report burn ban violations</li>\n</ul>\n<p>*<em>When using our online form, you will be asked to sign in or create an account on our customer service system.</em></p>\n\n<!-- <li>View or download a <a href=\"https://cdn.kingcounty.gov/-/media/king-county/depts/local-services/permits/fire-safety/king-county-fireworks-ban-faq-2023.pdf?rev=100480a3414c459fb326094022a72dce&amp;hash=4B36A23B307FEAFC04DC2DF690C9EB8C\" class=\"link-icon-download\">question-and-answer sheet</a>&nbsp;about the fireworks ban.</li> --></div>\r\n            </div>\r\n\r\n\r\n            <div class=\"col-sm-2 highlight-banner-link\">\r\n\r\n                \r\n            </div>\r\n\r\n        </div>\r\n    </div>\r\n</aside>\r\n\r\n\r\n<div class=\" pt-medium pb-medium border-bottom-overcast \">\r\n    <div class=\"two-col-container container\">\r\n        <div class=\"row\">\r\n            <div class=\"col-md-12 col-lg-8\">\r\n                \r\n<span id=\"Stage-1-burn-ban\" data-toc-anchor=\"Stage 1 burn ban\"></span>\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\n<div class=\"rich-text\"><h2 id=\"stage1\">Stage 1 Fire Safety Burn Ban</h2>\n<p class=\"h3\"><span class=\"material-icons\" style=\"vertical-align: -7px; font-size: 1.3em; color: #ce4a2e; \">dangerous</span> DO NOT burn (in rural areas):</p>\n<ul style=\"font-size: 1.25em;\">\n    <li>Yard debris*</li>\n    <li>To clear land</li>\n</ul>\n<p>*State regulation prohibits the burning of yard debris in urban areas at all times, regardless of whether a ban has been issued</p>\n\n<p class=\"h3\"><span class=\"material-icons\" style=\"vertical-align: -7px; font-size: 1.3em; color: #29856b; \">check_circle</span> OK to burn:</p>\n\n<ul style=\"font-size: 1.25em;\">\n    <li>Recreational fires* <span style=\"font-size: 0.8em;\">(campfires or fires in backyard fire pits, using approved firewood for outdoor recreation, ceremonial uses, or cooking)</span></li>\n</ul>\n<p>*May require a permit—contact your local fire district</p></div>\r\n\r\n<div id=\"accordion-e3a7c19854144c06914e835e35694195\" class=\"accordion\">\r\n    <h2 class=''></h2>\r\n\r\n        <div class=\"card\">\r\n            <div class=\"card-header\" id=\"accordion-e3a7c19854144c06914e835e35694195-1-ariaLabel\">\r\n                <h3 class='mb-0 h5'>\r\n                    <button class=\"btn btn-link collapsed\" data-toggle=\"collapse\" data-target=\"#accordion-e3a7c19854144c06914e835e35694195-1-dataTarget\" aria-expanded=\"true\" aria-controls=\"accordion-e3a7c19854144c06914e835e35694195-1-dataTarget\">\r\n                        Rules for recreational fires\r\n\r\n                        <span class=\"material-icons scroll-down-arrow\" translate=\"no\">expand_more</span>\r\n                    </button>\r\n                </h3>\r\n            </div>\r\n            <div id=\"accordion-e3a7c19854144c06914e835e35694195-1-dataTarget\" class=\"collapse\" aria-labelledby=\"accordion-e3a7c19854144c06914e835e35694195-1-ariaLabel\" data-parent=\"#accordion-e3a7c19854144c06914e835e35694195\">\r\n                <div class=\"card-body\"><ul>\n<li>Firewood must be <strong>seasoned and dry.</strong></li>\n<li>The fire must be <strong>built in a metal or concrete fire pit,</strong> like those typically found in campgrounds.</li>\n<li>The fire must <strong>not be used to dispose of debris.</strong></li>\n<li>The fire must grow <strong>no larger than three feet wide.</strong></li>\n<li>The fire must be in a clear spot, <strong>at least 10 feet away from all vegetation.</strong></li>\n<li>Overhanging branches must be <strong>at least 20 feet above the flames.</strong></li>\n<li>The fire must be at least <strong>25 feet away from any structure.</strong></li>\n<li>The fire must <strong>always be attended by an alert person with equipment</strong> that can put out the fire (such as a shovel and a five-gallon bucket of water, or a connected and charged water hose).</li>\n<li>After use, campfires <strong>must be completely extinguished</strong> by pouring water or moist soil over them and stirring with a shovel until all parts are cool to the touch.</li>\n<li>No burning is allowed when the <strong>wind speed is greater than 15 miles per hour.</strong></li>\n<li>If smoke or ash from the fire <strong>causes a nuisance to any neighbors,</strong> the fire must be extinguished.</li>\n</ul></div>\r\n\r\n            </div>\r\n        </div>\r\n</div>\r\n            </div>\r\n            <aside class=\"col-md-12 col-lg-4 right-rail\">\r\n                \r\n\r\n<aside class=\"vital-info-one-col\" aria-labelledby=\"vitalInfoHeading-367aebfcad584b4f978078564c41c284\">\r\n    <div class=\"vital-wrapper\" id=\"vitalInfoHeading-367aebfcad584b4f978078564c41c284\">\r\n        <h2 class='vital-info-heading'>CONTACT THE FIRE MARSHAL</h2>\r\n        \r\n        <div class=\"rich-text\"><p><a href=\"mailto:eurban@kingcounty.gov\">Eric Urban</a><br />\n206-263-3257</p></div>\r\n    </div>\r\n</aside>\r\n            </aside>\r\n        </div>\r\n    </div>\r\n</div>\r\n\r\n\r\n<div class=\" pt-medium pb-medium border-bottom-overcast \">\r\n    <div class=\"two-col-container container\">\r\n        <div class=\"row\">\r\n            <div class=\"col-md-12 col-lg-8\">\r\n                \r\n<span id=\"Stage-2-burn-ban\" data-toc-anchor=\"Stage 2 burn ban\"></span>\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\n<div class=\"rich-text\"><h2 id=\"stage2\">Stage 2 Fire Safety Burn Ban</h2>\n<p>Issued when fire danger is critically high</p>\n<p class=\"h3\"><span class=\"material-icons\" style=\"vertical-align: -8px; font-size: 1.5em; color: #ce4a2e;\">dangerous</span> DO NOT burn:\n</p>\n<ul style=\"font-size: 1.25em;\">\n    <li>ANY outdoor fires, including recreational and ceremonial fires</li>\n</ul>\n<p class=\"h3\"><span class=\"material-icons\" style=\"vertical-align: -7px; font-size: 1.3em; color: #29856b;\">check_circle</span> OK to use:\n</p>\n<p style=\"font-size: 1.25em;\">Manufactured portable outdoor devices* like&hellip;</p>\n<ul style=\"font-size: 1.25em;\">\n    <li>Gas and charcoal grills</li>\n    <li>Natural gas or propane appliances like patio warmers</li>\n    <li>Wood pellet smokers</li>\n</ul>\n<p>*Follow manufacturer&rsquo;s recommendations</p>\n<p><strong>Used coals must be disposed of properly:</strong>\n</p>\n<ol>\n    <li>Douse with water.</li>\n    <li>When cool enough to handle, put the coals into a metal container.</li>\n    <li>Seal the container with a tight-fitting lid.</li>\n    <li>After the coals have thoroughly cooled, they may be put into a regular trash bin.</li>\n</ol></div>\r\n            </div>\r\n            <aside class=\"col-md-12 col-lg-4 right-rail\">\r\n                \r\n            </aside>\r\n        </div>\r\n    </div>\r\n</div>\r\n\r\n\r\n<div class=\" pt-medium pb-medium border-bottom-overcast \">\r\n    <div class=\"two-col-container container\">\r\n        <div class=\"row\">\r\n            <div class=\"col-md-12 col-lg-8\">\r\n                \r\n<span id=\"When-there-is-no-burn-ban\" data-toc-anchor=\"When there is no burn ban\"></span>\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\n<div class=\"rich-text\"><h2 id=\"no-ban\">When there is no Fire&nbsp;Safety Burn&nbsp;Ban</h2>\n<p>The following restrictions still apply to outdoor burning in unincorporated King County, even when there is no fire safety burn ban.</p>\n<ul>\n    <li>Burning is restricted to property outside the urban growth boundary.</li>\n    <li>Fires to burn vegetation on residential property may only burn vegetation from the same property, and the fire can be no larger than 4x4x3 feet.</li>\n    <li>Contact your local fire department to find out if you need a permit for your fire (recreational fires are exempt).</li>\n</ul>\n<p><a href=\"https://pscleanair.gov/\" class=\"link-icon-outbound\">Puget Sound Clean Air Agency</a> regulations still apply to limit environmental, health, and pollution problems.</p>\n<ul>\n    <li>Air quality burn bans may be issued even though there is no fire safety burn ban.</li>\n    <li>Burning to clear land for development (i.e., building a new structure or subdivision), is prohibited.</li>\n    <li>Burning garbage, paper, or other trash is strictly prohibited at all times.</li>\n</ul></div>\r\n            </div>\r\n            <aside class=\"col-md-12 col-lg-4 right-rail\">\r\n                \r\n<div class=\"box bg-overcast\">\r\n\r\n    \r\n    <h2 class=\"h3\">Look up your local fire district</h2>\n\n<p><strong>Enter your address in <a href=\"https://gismaps.kingcounty.gov/parcelviewer2/\">King County’s Parcel Viewer</a>.</strong> A box will appear with your parcel number and other information about your property. Click on “Districts Report” and look under “King County Electoral districts” for “Fire district.”</p>\r\n</div>\r\n\r\n\r\n            </aside>\r\n        </div>\r\n    </div>\r\n</div>\r\n\r\n\r\n<div class=\" pt-medium pb-medium border-bottom-overcast \">\r\n    <div class=\"two-col-container container\">\r\n        <div class=\"row\">\r\n            <div class=\"col-md-12 col-lg-8\">\r\n                \r\n<span id=\"Check-other-agencies\" data-toc-anchor=\"Check other agencies\"></span>\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\n<div class=\"rich-text\"><h2 id=\"other-agencies\">Check other agencies</h2>\n<ul>\n    <li><a href=\"https://parks.wa.gov/about/news-announcements/alerts\" class=\"link-icon-outbound\">Washington State Parks – alerts page</a><br />\n    Shows burn ban information for all Washington State Parks.</li>\n    <li><a href=\"https://dnr.wa.gov/wildfire-resources/outdoor-burning/burn-restrictions\" class=\"link-icon-outbound\">Washington State Department of Natural Resources – burn restrictions</a><br />\n   Current information about wildfire danger in Washington and outdoor burning restrictions on Department of Natural Resources-protected forestlands throughout the&nbsp;state.</li>\n</ul></div>\r\n            </div>\r\n            <aside class=\"col-md-12 col-lg-4 right-rail\">\r\n                \r\n            </aside>\r\n        </div>\r\n    </div>\r\n</div>\r\n\r\n\r\n<div class=\" pt-medium pb-medium  \">\r\n    <div class=\"two-col-container container\">\r\n        <div class=\"row\">\r\n            <div class=\"col-md-12 col-lg-8\">\r\n                \r\n<span id=\"Air-quality-burn-bans\" data-toc-anchor=\"Air quality burn bans\"></span>\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\n\r\n<div class=\"rich-text\"><h2 id=\"air-quality-bans\">Air Quality Burn Bans</h2>\n<p>Another kind of burn ban, <strong>Air Quality Burn Bans</strong>, are issued and enforced by the Puget Sound Clean Air Agency to reduce environmental, health, and pollution problems. For current Air Quality Burn Ban status, visit the <a href=\"https://pscleanair.gov/\" class=\"link-icon-outbound\">Puget Sound Clean Air Agency website</a> or call 1&#8209;800&#8209;595&#8209;4341.</p></div>\r\n            </div>\r\n            <aside class=\"col-md-12 col-lg-4 right-rail\">\r\n                \r\n            </aside>\r\n        </div>\r\n    </div>\r\n</div>\r\n\r\n\r\n<section class=\"topic-cards-row\">\r\n    <div class=\"container\">\r\n        <h2>\r\n            Related Topics\r\n        </h2>\r\n        <div class=\"topic-cards-wrapper\">\r\n                <div class=\"topic-card\">\r\n                    <a href=\"/en/dept/local-services/buildings-property/inspections/building-inspections\">\r\n\r\n                    <div class=\"topic-card-header\">\r\n                        <h3 class=\"topic-card-title\">Fire inspections</h3>\r\n                    </div>\r\n                    <div class=\"topic-card-footer\">For residential and commercial buildings under construction</div>\r\n\r\n\r\n                    </a>\r\n                </div>\r\n                <div class=\"topic-card\">\r\n                    <a href=\"/en/dept/local-services/certificates-permits-licenses/permits/permits-inspections-codes-buildings-land-use/permit-forms-application-materials/fire-permits\">\r\n\r\n                    <div class=\\\"  pb-medium border-bottom-overcast \\\">\\r\\n    <div class=\\\"two-col-container container\\\">\\r\\n        <div class=\\\"row\\\">\\r\\n            <div class=\\\"col-md-12 col-lg-8\\\">\\r\\n                \\r\\n<header class=\\\"container\\\">\\r\\n    <div class=\\\"page-heading\\\">\\r\\n<h1>Fire Safety Burn Bans</h1><div class=\\\"summary-text\\\"><p>The King County Fire Marshal issues <strong>Fire Safety Burn Bans</strong> for unincorporated King County in response to hot and/or dry conditions, in coordination with King County cities and other county and state agencies.</p></div>    </div>\\r\\n</header><div id=\\\"toc-links\\\"></div>\\r\\n            </div>\\r\\n            <aside class=\\\"col-md-12 col-lg-4 right-rail\\\">\\r\\n                \\r\\n<div class=\\\"box bg-overcast\\\">\\r\\n\\r\\n    \\r\\n    <!-- STAGE 1 BURN BAN -->\\n<!-- updated 4/22/2024 by anna.clemenger@kingcounty.gov -->\\n<div style=\\\"text-align: center;\\\">\\n<p>Current status:<br />\\n<span class=\\\"h3\\\"><a href=\\\"#toc-Stage-2-burn-ban\\\" style=\\\"text-decoration: none; color: #ce4a2e;\\\"><strong>Stage 2 Fire Safety Burn Ban</strong></a></span></p>\\n<p><a href=\\\"#toc-Stage-2-burn-ban\\\">Restrictions apply</a></p>\\n</div>\\r\\n</div>\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n<div class=\\\"rich-text\\\"><p><strong>Related documents:</strong></p>\\n<p><a href=\\\"https://cdn.kingcounty.gov/-/media/king-county/depts/local-services/permits/building-land-use-permits/f/fire-hazards-vegetation-or-exterior-property-areas.pdf?rev=193c1949f94c4ef7b17128572d610ff2&amp;hash=D058A0CC245A47FC69B3723D061DFED3\\\" class=\\\"link-emphasis link-icon-picture_as_pdf\\\">Fire hazards &ndash; vegetation or exterior property areas</a></p></div>\\r\\n            </aside>\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n</div>\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n        <div class=\\\"\\\">\\r\\n            \\r\\n\\r\\n<aside class=\\\"highlight-banner highlight-banner-primary\\\" aria-labelledby=\\\"The-sale-and-use-of-consumer-fireworks-are-prohibited-in-unincorporated-King-County-title\\\">\\r\\n    <div class=\\\"container\\\">\\r\\n        <div class=\\\"row align-items-center\\\">\\r\\n\\r\\n                        <h2 class=\\\"col-sm-4 highlight-banner-title h3\\\" id=\\\"The-sale-and-use-of-consumer-fireworks-are-prohibited-in-unincorporated-King-County-title\\\">\\r\\n                            The sale and use of consumer fireworks are prohibited in unincorporated King County\\r\\n                        </h2>\\r\\n\\r\\n            <div class=\\\"col highlight-banner-text\\\">\\r\\n                <div class=\\\"rich-text\\\"><strong><a href=\\\"https://dls-permit.dynamics365portals.us/submit-case/\\\">Report fireworks or burning violations online</a>*</strong> or call:\\n<ul>\\n<li><strong>206-848-0800</strong> to report fireworks violations</li>\\n<li><strong>206-296-6675</strong> to report burn ban violations</li>\\n</ul>\\n<p>*<em>When using our online form, you will be asked to sign in or create an account on our customer service system.</em></p>\\n\\n<!-- <li>View or download a <a href=\\\"https://cdn.kingcounty.gov/-/media/king-county/depts/local-services/permits/fire-safety/king-county-fireworks-ban-faq-2023.pdf?rev=100480a3414c459fb326094022a72dce&amp;hash=4B36A23B307FEAFC04DC2DF690C9EB8C\\\" class=\\\"link-icon-download\\\">question-and-answer sheet</a>&nbsp;about the fireworks ban.</li> --></div>\\r\\n            </div>\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n            <div class=\\\"col-sm-2 highlight-banner-link\\\">\\r\\n\\r\\n                \\r\\n            </div>\\r\\n\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n</aside>\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n<div class=\\\" pt-medium pb-medium border-bottom-overcast \\\">\\r\\n    <div class=\\\"two-col-container container\\\">\\r\\n        <div class=\\\"row\\\">\\r\\n            <div class=\\\"col-md-12 col-lg-8\\\">\\r\\n                \\r\\n<span id=\\\"Stage-1-burn-ban\\\" data-toc-anchor=\\\"Stage 1 burn ban\\\"></span>\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n<div class=\\\"rich-text\\\"><h2 id=\\\"stage1\\\">Stage 1 Fire Safety Burn Ban</h2>\\n<p class=\\\"h3\\\"><span class=\\\"material-icons\\\" style=\\\"vertical-align: -7px; font-size: 1.3em; color: #ce4a2e; \\\">dangerous</span> DO NOT burn (in rural areas):</p>\\n<ul style=\\\"font-size: 1.25em;\\\">\\n    <li>Yard debris*</li>\\n    <li>To clear land</li>\\n</ul>\\n<p>*State regulation prohibits the burning of yard debris in urban areas at all times, regardless of whether a ban has been issued</p>\\n\\n<p class=\\\"h3\\\"><span class=\\\"material-icons\\\" style=\\\"vertical-align: -7px; font-size: 1.3em; color: #29856b; \\\">check_circle</span> OK to burn:</p>\\n\\n<ul style=\\\"font-size: 1.25em;\\\">\\n    <li>Recreational fires* <span style=\\\"font-size: 0.8em;\\\">(campfires or fires in backyard fire pits, using approved firewood for outdoor recreation, ceremonial uses, or cooking)</span></li>\\n</ul>\\n<p>*May require a permit—contact your local fire district</p></div>\\r\\n\\r\\n<div id=\\\"accordion-e3a7c19854144c06914e835e35694195\\\" class=\\\"accordion\\\">\\r\\n    <h2 class=''></h2>\\r\\n\\r\\n        <div class=\\\"card\\\">\\r\\n            <div class=\\\"card-header\\\" id=\\\"accordion-e3a7c19854144c06914e835e35694195-1-ariaLabel\\\">\\r\\n                <h3 class='mb-0 h5'>\\r\\n                    <button class=\\\"btn btn-link collapsed\\\" data-toggle=\\\"collapse\\\" data-target=\\\"#accordion-e3a7c19854144c06914e835e35694195-1-dataTarget\\\" aria-expanded=\\\"true\\\" aria-controls=\\\"accordion-e3a7c19854144c06914e835e35694195-1-dataTarget\\\">\\r\\n                        Rules for recreational fires\\r\\n\\r\\n                        <span class=\\\"material-icons scroll-down-arrow\\\" translate=\\\"no\\\">expand_more</span>\\r\\n                    </button>\\r\\n                </h3>\\r\\n            </div>\\r\\n            <div id=\\\"accordion-e3a7c19854144c06914e835e35694195-1-dataTarget\\\" class=\\\"collapse\\\" aria-labelledby=\\\"accordion-e3a7c19854144c06914e835e35694195-1-ariaLabel\\\" data-parent=\\\"#accordion-e3a7c19854144c06914e835e35694195\\\">\\r\\n                <div class=\\\"card-body\\\"><ul>\\n<li>Firewood must be <strong>seasoned and dry.</strong></li>\\n<li>The fire must be <strong>built in a metal or concrete fire pit,</strong> like those typically found in campgrounds.</li>\\n<li>The fire must <strong>not be used to dispose of debris.</strong></li>\\n<li>The fire must grow <strong>no larger than three feet wide.</strong></li>\\n<li>The fire must be in a clear spot, <strong>at least 10 feet away from all vegetation.</strong></li>\\n<li>Overhanging branches must be <strong>at least 20 feet above the flames.</strong></li>\\n<li>The fire must be at least <strong>25 feet away from any structure.</strong></li>\\n<li>The fire must <strong>always be attended by an alert person with equipment</strong> that can put out the fire (such as a shovel and a five-gallon bucket of water, or a connected and charged water hose).</li>\\n<li>After use, campfires <strong>must be completely extinguished</strong> by pouring water or moist soil over them and stirring with a shovel until all parts are cool to the touch.</li>\\n<li>No burning is allowed when the <strong>wind speed is greater than 15 miles per hour.</strong></li>\\n<li>If smoke or ash from the fire <strong>causes a nuisance to any neighbors,</strong> the fire must be extinguished.</li>\\n</ul></div>\\r\\n\\r\\n            </div>\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n</div>\\r\\n            </div>\\r\\n            <aside class=\\\"col-md-12 col-lg-4 right-rail\\\">\\r\\n                \\r\\n\\r\\n<aside class=\\\"vital-info-one-col\\\" aria-labelledby=\\\"vitalInfoHeading-367aebfcad584b4f978078564c41c284\\\">\\r\\n    <div class=\\\"vital-wrapper\\\" id=\\\"vitalInfoHeading-367aebfcad584b4f978078564c41c284\\\">\\r\\n        <h2 class='vital-info-heading'>CONTACT THE FIRE MARSHAL</h2>\\r\\n        \\r\\n        <div class=\\\"rich-text\\\"><p><a href=\\\"mailto:eurban@kingcounty.gov\\\">Eric Urban</a><br />\\n206-263-3257</p></div>\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n</aside>\\r\\n            </aside>\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n</div>\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n<div class=\\\" pt-medium pb-medium border-bottom-overcast \\\">\\r\\n    <div class=\\\"two-col-container container\\\">\\r\\n        <div class=\\\"row\\\">\\r\\n            <div class=\\\"col-md-12 col-lg-8\\\">\\r\\n                \\r\\n<span id=\\\"Stage-2-burn-ban\\\" data-toc-anchor=\\\"Stage 2 burn ban\\\"></span>\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n<div class=\\\"rich-text\\\"><h2 id=\\\"stage2\\\">Stage 2 Fire Safety Burn Ban</h2>\\n<p>Issued when fire danger is critically high</p>\\n<p class=\\\"h3\\\"><span class=\\\"material-icons\\\" style=\\\"vertical-align: -8px; font-size: 1.5em; color: #ce4a2e;\\\">dangerous</span> DO NOT burn:\\n</p>\\n<ul style=\\\"font-size: 1.25em;\\\">\\n    <li>ANY outdoor fires, including recreational and ceremonial fires</li>\\n</ul>\\n<p class=\\\"h3\\\"><span class=\\\"material-icons\\\" style=\\\"vertical-align: -7px; font-size: 1.3em; color: #29856b;\\\">check_circle</span> OK to use:\\n</p>\\n<p style=\\\"font-size: 1.25em;\\\">Manufactured portable outdoor devices* like&hellip;</p>\\n<ul style=\\\"font-size: 1.25em;\\\">\\n    <li>Gas and charcoal grills</li>\\n    <li>Natural gas or propane appliances like patio warmers</li>\\n    <li>Wood pellet smokers</li>\\n</ul>\\n<p>*Follow manufacturer&rsquo;s recommendations</p>\\n<p><strong>Used coals must be disposed of properly:</strong>\\n</p>\\n<ol>\\n    <li>Douse with water.</li>\\n    <li>When cool enough to handle, put the coals into a metal container.</li>\\n    <li>Seal the container with a tight-fitting lid.</li>\\n    <li>After the coals have thoroughly cooled, they may be put into a regular trash bin.</li>\\n</ol></div>\\r\\n            </div>\\r\\n            <aside class=\\\"col-md-12 col-lg-4 right-rail\\\">\\r\\n                \\r\\n            </aside>\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n</div>\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n<div class=\\\" pt-medium pb-medium border-bottom-overcast \\\">\\r\\n    <div class=\\\"two-col-container container\\\">\\r\\n        <div class=\\\"row\\\">\\r\\n            <div class=\\\"col-md-12 col-lg-8\\\">\\r\\n                \\r\\n<span id=\\\"When-there-is-no-burn-ban\\\" data-toc-anchor=\\\"When there is no burn ban\\\"></span>\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n<div class=\\\"rich-text\\\"><h2 id=\\\"no-ban\\\">When there is no Fire&nbsp;Safety Burn&nbsp;Ban</h2>\\n<p>The following restrictions still apply to outdoor burning in unincorporated King County, even when there is no fire safety burn ban.</p>\\n<ul>\\n    <li>Burning is restricted to property outside the urban growth boundary.</li>\\n    <li>Fires to burn vegetation on residential property may only burn vegetation from the same property, and the fire can be no larger than 4x4x3 feet.</li>\\n    <li>Contact your local fire department to find out if you need a permit for your fire (recreational fires are exempt).</li>\\n</ul>\\n<p><a href=\\\"https://pscleanair.gov/\\\" class=\\\"link-icon-outbound\\\">Puget Sound Clean Air Agency</a> regulations still apply to limit environmental, health, and pollution problems.</p>\\n<ul>\\n    <li>Air quality burn bans may be issued even though there is no fire safety burn ban.</li>\\n    <li>Burning to clear land for development (i.e., building a new structure or subdivision), is prohibited.</li>\\n    <li>Burning garbage, paper, or other trash is strictly prohibited at all times.</li>\\n</ul></div>\\r\\n            </div>\\r\\n            <aside class=\\\"col-md-12 col-lg-4 right-rail\\\">\\r\\n                \\r\\n<div class=\\\"box bg-overcast\\\">\\r\\n\\r\\n    \\r\\n    <h2 class=\\\"h3\\\">Look up your local fire district</h2>\\n\\n<p><strong>Enter your address in <a href=\\\"https://gismaps.kingcounty.gov/parcelviewer2/\\\">King County’s Parcel Viewer</a>.</strong> A box will appear with your parcel number and other information about your property. Click on “Districts Report” and look under “King County Electoral districts” for “Fire district.”</p>\\r\\n</div>\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n            </aside>\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n</div>\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n<div class=\\\" pt-medium pb-medium border-bottom-overcast \\\">\\r\\n    <div class=\\\"two-col-container container\\\">\\r\\n        <div class=\\\"row\\\">\\r\\n            <div class=\\\"col-md-12 col-lg-8\\\">\\r\\n                \\r\\n<span id=\\\"Check-other-agencies\\\" data-toc-anchor=\\\"Check other agencies\\\"></span>\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n<div class=\\\"rich-text\\\"><h2 id=\\\"other-agencies\\\">Check other agencies</h2>\\n<ul>\\n    <li><a href=\\\"https://parks.wa.gov/about/news-announcements/alerts\\\" class=\\\"link-icon-outbound\\\">Washington State Parks – alerts page</a><br />\\n    Shows burn ban information for all Washington State Parks.</li>\\n    <li><a href=\\\"https://dnr.wa.gov/wildfire-resources/outdoor-burning/burn-restrictions\\\" class=\\\"link-icon-outbound\\\">Washington State Department of Natural Resources – burn restrictions</a><br />\\n   Current information about wildfire danger in Washington and outdoor burning restrictions on Department of Natural Resources-protected forestlands throughout the&nbsp;state.</li>\\n</ul></div>\\r\\n            </div>\\r\\n            <aside class=\\\"col-md-12 col-lg-4 right-rail\\\">\\r\\n                \\r\\n            </aside>\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n</div>\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n<div class=\\\" pt-medium pb-medium  \\\">\\r\\n    <div class=\\\"two-col-container container\\\">\\r\\n        <div class=\\\"row\\\">\\r\\n            <div class=\\\"col-md-12 col-lg-8\\\">\\r\\n                \\r\\n<span id=\\\"Air-quality-burn-bans\\\" data-toc-anchor=\\\"Air quality burn bans\\\"></span>\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n<div class=\\\"rich-text\\\"><h2 id=\\\"air-quality-bans\\\">Air Quality Burn Bans</h2>\\n<p>Another kind of burn ban, <strong>Air Quality Burn Bans</strong>, are issued and enforced by the Puget Sound Clean Air Agency to reduce environmental, health, and pollution problems. For current Air Quality Burn Ban status, visit the <a href=\\\"https://pscleanair.gov/\\\" class=\\\"link-icon-outbound\\\">Puget Sound Clean Air Agency website</a> or call 1&#8209;800&#8209;595&#8209;4341.</p></div>\\r\\n            </div>\\r\\n            <aside class=\\\"col-md-12 col-lg-4 right-rail\\\">\\r\\n                \\r\\n            </aside>\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n</div>\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n<section class=\\\"topic-cards-row\\\">\\r\\n    <div class=\\\"container\\\">\\r\\n        <h2>\\r\\n            Related Topics\\r\\n               <li class=\\\"breadcrumb-item\\\">\\r\\n                                <a href=\\\"/en/dept/local-services\\\">Local Services</a>\\r\\n\\r\\n                    </li>\\r\\n                    <li class=\\\"breadcrumb-item\\\">\\r\\n                                <span>Fire Safety Burn Bans</span>\\r\\n\\r\\n                    </li>\\r\\n        </ol>\\r\\n    </nav>\\r\\n</div>\\r\\n    <main id=\\\"maincontent\\\" class=\\\"add-scroll-top-margin\\\">\\r\\n\\r\\n        <div class=\\\"\\\">\\r\\n            \\r\\n            \\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n<div class=\\\"  pb-medium border-bottom-overcast \\\">\\r\\n    <div class=\\\"two-col-container container\\\">\\r\\n        <div class=\\\"row\\\">\\r\\n            <div class=\\\"col-md-12 col-lg-8\\\">\\r\\n                \\r\\n<header class=\\\"container\\\">\\r\\n    <div class=\\\"page-heading\\\">\\r\\n<h1>Fire Safety Burn Bans</h1><div class=\\\"summary-text\\\"><p>The King County Fire Marshal issues <strong>Fire Safety Burn Bans</strong> for unincorporated King County in response to hot and/or dry conditions, in coordination with King County cities and other county and state agencies.</p></div>    </div>\\r\\n</header><div id=\\\"toc-links\\\"></div>\\r\\n            </div>\\r\\n            <aside class=\\\"col-md-12 col-lg-4 right-rail\\\">\\r\\n                \\r\\n<div class=\\\"box bg-overcast\\\">\\r\\n\\r\\n    \\r\\n    <!-- STAGE 1 BURN BAN -->\\n<!-- updated 4/22/2024 by anna.clemenger@kingcounty.gov -->\\n<div style=\\\"text-align: center;\\\">\\n<p>Current status:<br />\\n<span class=\\\"h3\\\"><a href=\\\"#toc-Stage-2-burn-ban\\\" style=\\\"text-decoration: none; color: #ce4a2e;\\\"><strong>Stage 2 Fire Safety Burn Ban</strong></a></span></p>\\n<p><a href=\\\"#toc-Stage-2-burn-ban\\\">Restrictions apply</a></p>\\n</div>\\r\\n</div>\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n<div class=\\\"rich-text\\\"><p><strong>Related documents:</strong></p>\\n<p><a href=\\\"https://cdn.kingcounty.gov/-/media/king-county/depts/local-services/permits/building-land-use-permits/f/fire-hazards-vegetation-or-exterior-property-areas.pdf?rev=193c1949f94c4ef7b17128572d610ff2&amp;hash=D058A0CC245A47FC69B3723D061DFED3\\\" class=\\\"link-emphasis link-icon-picture_as_pdf\\\">Fire hazards &ndash; vegetation or exterior property areas</a></p></div>\\r\\n            </aside>\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n</div>\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n        <div class=\\\"\\\">\\r\\n            \\r\\n\\r\\n<aside class=\\\"highlight-banner highlight-banner-primary\\\" aria-labelledby=\\\"The-sale-and-use-of-consumer-fireworks-are-prohibited-in-unincorporated-King-County-title\\\">\\r\\n    <div class=\\\"container\\\">\\r\\n        <div class=\\\"row align-items-center\\\">\\r\\n\\r\\n                        <h2 class=\\\"col-sm-4 highlight-banner-title h3\\\" id=\\\"The-sale-and-use-of-consumer-fireworks-are-prohibited-in-unincorporated-King-County-title\\\">\\r\\n                            The sale and use of consumer fireworks are prohibited in unincorporated King County\\r\\n                        </h2>\\r\\n\\r\\n            <div class=\\\"col highlight-banner-text\\\">\\r\\n                <div class=\\\"rich-text\\\"><strong><a href=\\\"https://dls-permit.dynamics365portals.us/submit-case/\\\">Report fireworks or burning violations online</a>*</strong> or call:\\n<ul>\\n<li><strong>206-848-0800</strong> to report fireworks violations</li>\\n<li><strong>206-296-6675</strong> to report burn ban violations</li>\\n</ul>\\n<p>*<em>When using our online form, you will be asked to sign in or create an account on our customer service system.</em></p>\\n\\n<!-- <li>View or download a <a href=\\\"https://cdn.kingcounty.gov/-/media/king-county/depts/local-services/permits/fire-safety/king-county-fireworks-ban-faq-2023.pdf?rev=100480a3414c459fb326094022a72dce&amp;hash=4B36A23B307FEAFC04DC2DF690C9EB8C\\\" class=\\\"link-icon-download\\\">question-and-answer sheet</a>&nbsp;about the fireworks ban.</li> --></div>\\r\\n            </div>\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n            <div class=\\\"col-sm-2 highlight-banner-link\\\">\\r\\n\\r\\n                \\r\\n            </div>\\r\\n\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n</aside>\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n<div class=\\\" pt-medium pb-medium border-bottom-overcast \\\">\\r\\n    <div class=\\\"two-col-container container\\\">\\r\\n        <div class=\\\"row\\\">\\r\\n            <div class=\\\"col-md-12 col-lg-8\\\">\\r\\n                \\r\\n<span id=\\\"Stage-1-burn-ban\\\" data-toc-anchor=\\\"Stage 1 burn ban\\\"></span>\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n<div class=\\\"rich-text\\\"><h2 id=\\\"stage1\\\">Stage 1 Fire Safety Burn Ban</h2>\\n<p class=\\\"h3\\\"><span class=\\\"material-icons\\\" style=\\\"vertical-align: -7px; font-size: 1.3em; color: #ce4a2e; \\\">dangerous</span> DO NOT burn (in rural areas):</p>\\n<ul style=\\\"font-size: 1.25em;\\\">\\n    <li>Yard debris*</li>\\n    <li>To clear land</li>\\n</ul>\\n<p>*State regulation prohibits the burning of yard debris in urban areas at all times, regardless of whether a ban has been issued</p>\\n\\n<p class=\\\"h3\\\"><span class=\\\"material-icons\\\" style=\\\"vertical-align: -7px; font-size: 1.3em; color: #29856b; \\\">check_circle</span> OK to burn:</p>\\n\\n<ul style=\\\"font-size: 1.25em;\\\">\\n    <li>Recreational fires* <span style=\\\"font-size: 0.8em;\\\">(campfires or fires in backyard fire pits, using approved firewood for outdoor recreation, ceremonial uses, or cooking)</span></li>\\n</ul>\\n<p>*May require a permit—contact your local fire district</p></div>\\r\\n\\r\\n<div id=\\\"accordion-e3a7c19854144c06914e835e35694195\\\" class=\\\"accordion\\\">\\r\\n    <h2 class=''></h2>\\r\\n\\r\\n        <div class=\\\"card\\\">\\r\\n            <div class=\\\"card-header\\\" id=\\\"accordion-e3a7c19854144c06914e835e35694195-1-ariaLabel\\\">\\r\\n                <h3 class='mb-0 h5'>\\r\\n                    <button class=\\\"btn btn-link collapsed\\\" data-toggle=\\\"collapse\\\" data-target=\\\"#accordion-e3a7c19854144c06914e835e35694195-1-dataTarget\\\" aria-expanded=\\\"true\\\" aria-controls=\\\"accordion-e3a7c19854144c06914e835e35694195-1-dataTarget\\\">\\r\\n                        Rules for recreational fires\\r\\n\\r\\n                        <span class=\\\"material-icons scroll-down-arrow\\\" translate=\\\"no\\\">expand_more</span>\\r\\n                    </button>\\r\\n                </h3>\\r\\n            </div>\\r\\n            <div id=\\\"accordion-e3a7c19854144c06914e835e35694195-1-dataTarget\\\" class=\\\"collapse\\\" aria-labelledby=\\\"accordion-e3a7c19854144c06914e835e35694195-1-ariaLabel\\\" data-parent=\\\"#accordion-e3a7c19854144c06914e835e35694195\\\">\\r\\n                <div class=\\\"card-body\\\"><ul>\\n<li>Firewood must be <strong>seasoned and dry.</strong></li>\\n<li>The fire must be <strong>built in a metal or concrete fire pit,</strong> like those typically found in campgrounds.</li>\\n<li>The fire must <strong>not be used to dispose of debris.</strong></li>\\n<li>The fire must grow <strong>no larger than three feet wide.</strong></li>\\n<li>The fire must be in a clear spot, <strong>at least 10 feet away from all vegetation.</strong></li>\\n<li>Overhanging branches must be <strong>at least 20 feet above the flames.</strong></li>\\n<li>The fire must be at least <strong>25 feet away from any structure.</strong></li>\\n<li>The fire must <strong>always be attended by an alert person with equipment</strong> that can put out the fire (such as a shovel and a five-gallon bucket of water, or a connected and charged water hose).</li>\\n<li>After use, campfires <strong>must be completely extinguished</strong> by pouring water or moist soil over them and stirring with a shovel until all parts are cool to the touch.</li>\\n<li>No burning is allowed when the <strong>wind speed is greater than 15 miles per hour.</strong></li>\\n<li>If smoke or ash from the fire <strong>causes a nuisance to any neighbors,</strong> the fire must be extinguished.</li>\\n</ul></div>\\r\\n\\r\\n            </div>\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n</div>\\r\\n            </div>\\r\\n            <aside class=\\\"col-md-12 col-lg-4 right-rail\\\">\\r\\n                \\r\\n\\r\\n<aside class=\\\"vital-info-one-col\\\" aria-labelledby=\\\"vitalInfoHeading-367aebfcad584b4f978078564c41c284\\\">\\r\\n    <div class=\\\"vital-wrapper\\\" id=\\\"vitalInfoHeading-367aebfcad584b4f978078564c41c284\\\">\\r\\n        <h2 class='vital-info-heading'>CONTACT THE FIRE MARSHAL</h2>\\r\\n        \\r\\n        <div class=\\\"rich-text\\\"><p><a href=\\\"mailto:eurban@kingcounty.gov\\\">Eric Urban</a><br />\\n206-263-3257</p></div>\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n</aside>\\r\\n            </aside>\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n</div>\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n<div class=\\\" pt-medium pb-medium border-bottom-overcast \\\">\\r\\n    <div class=\\\"two-col-container container\\\">\\r\\n        <div class=\\\"row\\\">\\r\\n            <div class=\\\"col-md-12 col-lg-8\\\">\\r\\n                \\r\\n<span id=\\\"Stage-2-burn-ban\\\" data-toc-anchor=\\\"Stage 2 burn ban\\\"></span>\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n<div class=\\\"rich-text\\\"><h2 id=\\\"stage2\\\">Stage 2 Fire Safety Burn Ban</h2>\\n<p>Issued when fire danger is critically high</p>\\n<p class=\\\"h3\\\"><span class=\\\"material-icons\\\" style=\\\"vertical-align: -8px; font-size: 1.5em; color: #ce4a2e;\\\">dangerous</span> DO NOT burn:\\n</p>\\n<ul style=\\\"font-size: 1.25em;\\\">\\n    <li>ANY outdoor fires, including recreational and ceremonial fires</li>\\n</ul>\\n<p class=\\\"h3\\\"><span class=\\\"material-icons\\\" style=\\\"vertical-align: -7px; font-size: 1.3em; color: #29856b;\\\">check_circle</span> OK to use:\\n</p>\\n<p style=\\\"font-size: 1.25em;\\\">Manufactured portable outdoor devices* like&hellip;</p>\\n<ul style=\\\"font-size: 1.25em;\\\">\\n    <li>Gas and charcoal grills</li>\\n    <li>Natural gas or propane appliances like patio warmers</li>\\n    <li>Wood pellet smokers</li>\\n</ul>\\n<p>*Follow manufacturer&rsquo;s recommendations</p>\\n<p><strong>Used coals must be disposed of properly:</strong>\\n</p>\\n<ol>\\n    <li>Douse with water.</li>\\n    <li>When cool enough to handle, put the coals into a metal container.</li>\\n    <li>Seal the container with a tight-fitting lid.</li>\\n    <li>After the coals have thoroughly cooled, they may be put into a regular trash bin.</li>\\n</ol></div>\\r\\n            </div>\\r\\n            <aside class=\\\"col-md-12 col-lg-4 right-rail\\\">\\r\\n                \\r\\n            </aside>\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n</div>\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n<div class=\\\" pt-medium pb-medium border-bottom-overcast \\\">\\r\\n    <div class=\\\"two-col-container container\\\">\\r\\n        <div class=\\\"row\\\">\\r\\n            <div class=\\\"col-md-12 col-lg-8\\\">\\r\\n                \\r\\n<span id=\\\"When-there-is-no-burn-ban\\\" data-toc-anchor=\\\"When there is no burn ban\\\"></span>\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n<div class=\\\"rich-text\\\"><h2 id=\\\"no-ban\\\">When there is no Fire&nbsp;Safety Burn&nbsp;Ban</h2>\\n<p>The following restrictions still apply to outdoor burning in unincorporated King County, even when there is no fire safety burn ban.</p>\\n<ul>\\n    <li>Burning is restricted to property outside the urban growth boundary.</li>\\n    <li>Fires to burn vegetation on residential property may only burn vegetation from the same property, and the fire can be no larger than 4x4x3 feet.</li>\\n    <li>Contact your local fire department to find out if you need a permit for your fire (recreational fires are exempt).</li>\\n</ul>\\n<p><a href=\\\"https://pscleanair.gov/\\\" class=\\\"link-icon-outbound\\\">Puget Sound Clean Air Agency</a> regulations still apply to limit environmental, health, and pollution problems.</p>\\n<ul>\\n    <li>Air quality burn bans may be issued even though there is no fire safety burn ban.</li>\\n    <li>Burning to clear land for development (i.e., building a new structure or subdivision), is prohibited.</li>\\n    <li>Burning garbage, paper, or other trash is strictly prohibited at all times.</li>\\n</ul></div>\\r\\n            </div>\\r\\n            <aside class=\\\"col-md-12 col-lg-4 right-rail\\\">\\r\\n                \\r\\n<div class=\\\"box bg-overcast\\\">\\r\\n\\r\\n    \\r\\n    <h2 class=\\\"h3\\\">Look up your local fire district</h2>\\n\\n<p><strong>Enter your address in <a href=\\\"https://gismaps.kingcounty.gov/parcelviewer2/\\\">King County’s Parcel Viewer</a>.</strong> A box will appear with your parcel number and other information about your property. Click on “Districts Report” and look under “King County Electoral districts” for “Fire district.”</p>\\r\\n</div>\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n            </aside>\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n</div>\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n<div class=\\\" pt-medium pb-medium border-bottom-overcast \\\">\\r\\n    <div class=\\\"two-col-container container\\\">\\r\\n        <div class=\\\"row\\\">\\r\\n            <div class=\\\"col-md-12 col-lg-8\\\">\\r\\n                \\r\\n<span id=\\\"Check-other-agencies\\\" data-toc-anchor=\\\"Check other agencies\\\"></span>\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n<div class=\\\"rich-text\\\"><h2 id=\\\"other-agencies\\\">Check other agencies</h2>\\n<ul>\\n    <li><a href=\\\"https://parks.wa.gov/about/news-announcements/alerts\\\" class=\\\"link-icon-outbound\\\">Washington State Parks – alerts page</a><br />\\n    Shows burn ban information for all Washington State Parks.</li>\\n    <li><a href=\\\"https://dnr.wa.gov/wildfire-resources/outdoor-burning/burn-restrictions\\\" class=\\\"link-icon-outbound\\\">Washington State Department of Natural Resources – burn restrictions</a><br />\\n   Current information about wildfire danger in Washington and outdoor burning restrictions on Department of Natural Resources-protected forestlands throughout the&nbsp;state.</li>\\n</ul></div>\\r\\n            </div>\\r\\n            <aside class=\\\"col-md-12 col-lg-4 right-rail\\\">\\r\\n                \\r\\n            </aside>\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n</div>\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n<div class=\\\" pt-medium pb-medium  \\\">\\r\\n    <div class=\\\"two-col-container container\\\">\\r\\n        <div class=\\\"row\\\">\\r\\n            <div class=\\\"col-md-12 col-lg-8\\\">\\r\\n                \\r\\n<span id=\\\"Air-quality-burn-bans\\\" data-toc-anchor=\\\"Air quality burn bans\\\"></span>\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n<div class=\\\"rich-text\\\"><h2 id=\\\"air-quality-bans\\\">Air Quality Burn Bans</h2>\\n<p>Another kind of burn ban, <strong>Air Quality Burn Bans</strong>, are issued and enforced by the Puget Sound Clean Air Agency to reduce environmental, health, and pollution problems. For current Air Quality Burn Ban status, visit the <a href=\\\"https://pscleanair.gov/\\\" class=\\\"link-icon-outbound\\\">Puget Sound Clean Air Agency website</a> or call 1&#8209;800&#8209;595&#8209;4341.</p></div>\\r\\n            </div>\\r\\n            <aside class=\\\"col-md-12 col-lg-4 right-rail\\\">\\r\\n                \\r\\n            </aside>\\r\\n        </div>\\r\\n    </div>\\r\\n</div>\\r\\n\\r\\n\\r\\n<section class=\\\"topic-cards-row\\\">\\r\\n    <div class=\\\"container\\\">\\r\\n        <h2>\\r\\n            Related Topics\\r\\n
